- [medium] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:604-613 — `_commitStagedPdf` returns silently when `production == null`, leaving `_pdfPendingCommit` true and `_importedPdfPath` pointing at the temp staging copy; the caller `_acceptScript` then proceeds to save the script and push `/production` as if the PDF was committed — consequence: the production's `scriptPath` never points at the staged PDF, so "view page" later opens the previous script's PDF (wrong document shown) with no user-visible error; smallest safe fix: on the null-production branch set `_pdfPendingCommit = false`, clear `_importedPdfPath`, and surface an error toast instead of returning silently.
- [medium] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:621-622 — `File(staged).copy('$destPath.incoming')` then `rename(destPath)` is not atomic across the copy failure: if `copy` throws, `_commitStagedPdf`'s outer try (in `_acceptScript`) catches it, but `destPath.incoming` is left behind in `Documents/scripts/` and the previous script's PDF is untouched — consequence: orphaned `.incoming` files accumulate and the accept flow reports failure after the script was already saved (state persisted before terminal status); smallest safe fix: wrap copy+rename in try/catch that deletes `destPath.incoming` on failure before rethrowing.
- [medium] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:624-629 — after a successful rename, deleting the staged temp file failure is only logged, but if `File(staged).delete()` throws the staged copy remains in tmp forever (no cleanup on later runs) — consequence: temp-dir growth across sessions; smallest safe fix: also attempt deletion in a `finally` or on next import.
- [low] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:181-201 — `_countsFor` memoizes on `identical(cached.$1, script.lines)`; after `_openReview` replaces `_preview` with a new `ParsedScript` whose `lines` is a fresh list, the memo is correctly invalidated, but the tuple stores the lines list strongly, keeping the old list alive if a caller mutates it in place — consequence: minor retention only; smallest safe fix: none required.
- [low] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:436-452 — `_openReview` rebuilds `_preview` but does not reset `_previewCounts`; `_countsFor` then recomputes because the lines list identity changed — correct, no finding.
- [medium] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:570-597 — `_acceptScript` catches all errors and shows a snackbar, but `_commitStagedPdf`'s silent null-production return (604-613) and the staged-copy failure path in `_pickPdfFile` (684-702) both leave `_importedPdfPath`/`_pdfPendingCommit` inconsistent with what was saved — consequence: the accept flow can complete with the production pointing at a PDF that was never committed (silent data inconsistency reported as success); smallest safe fix: make `_commitStagedPdf` return a success boolean and have `_acceptScript` abort the save when it failed.
- [low] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:711-718 — `service.lastImportFailedPages` is read after the import completed; if the service resets it on a subsequent import the warning can describe the wrong run — consequence: stale warning text; smallest safe fix: capture the value immediately after `importFromPdf` returns.
- [low] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:665-735 — `WakelockPlus.enable()` is enabled before the OCR await and disabled in `finally`, but if `_pickPdfFile` is re-entered concurrently (double-tap on the import button while `_loading` is true — the buttons are not disabled during loading) the second call's `finally` disables the wakelock while the first OCR is still running — consequence: screen can sleep mid-import on the first run; smallest safe fix: guard the pick handlers with `if (_loading) return;` or use a counter.
- [low] lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart:284-291 — the "Re-import" button clears `_importedPdfPath` and `_pdfPendingCommit` but does not delete the staged temp file `import.staged.pdf` — consequence: leftover temp file until the next import overwrites it; smallest safe fix: `delete` best-effort before clearing.

## Coverage
lib/features/script_import/pdf_page_view.dart — findings: 5
lib/features/script_import/script_import_screen.dart — findings: 7
- [medium] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:246-268 — `_buildLiveAsrTile` computes `totalBytes` from `models` but iterates `states` (a map keyed by the same models, so currently equal); if any model is missing from `availableModels` at build time the weighted progress silently mis-sums — consequence: progress bar can exceed 1.0 or stall at 0 — smallest safe fix: compute `totalBytes` from `states.keys` (or assert both lists match) so the denominator always matches the numerator.
- [medium] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:331-336 — delete loop `for (final m in models) await _service.delete(m.id)` has no error handling; a failed delete aborts the remaining files and the UI still shows the group as partially installed with no feedback — consequence: user believes group deleted while stale files remain on disk — smallest safe fix: wrap each delete in try/catch, collect failures, and surface a toast listing files that failed to delete.
- [medium] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:109-118 — `_deleteOnnxKokoro` awaits `clearCache()` with no try/catch; a thrown error leaves `_onnxReady` true (state never reset) and no user-visible failure — consequence: tile keeps showing "Installed" after a failed delete, and download button stays hidden — smallest safe fix: wrap in try/catch, set `_onnxReady = false` only on success, and toast the error on failure.
- [low] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:424-435 — `_download` awaits `_service.download(model)` then re-reads state; if the service throws, the uncaught exception propagates out of an IconButton onPressed with no toast — consequence: silent failure with no feedback (relies on service to catch internally; if it does not, UI just does nothing) — smallest safe fix: wrap in try/catch and show the existing error toast on exception.
- [low] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:54-106 — `_downloadOnnxKokoro` catches errors but never resets `_onnxProgress`; a failed download leaves the stale progress value shown next time `_onnxDownloading` becomes true — consequence: progress bar starts at the previous run's percentage — smallest safe fix: reset `_onnxProgress = 0` at the start of `_downloadOnnxKokoro` (already sets `_onnxStatus`; add progress reset).
- [low] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:63-78 — `downloadKokoro` progress callback updates state via `setState` on every progress tick with no throttle; for a ~180 MB archive this fires hundreds of setState calls — consequence: excessive rebuilds of the whole screen during download — smallest safe fix: throttle progress updates (e.g. only setState when the integer percent changes).
- [low] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:223-225, 331-336, 406-408 — PopupMenuButton `onSelected` handlers call async delete functions without awaiting or error handling; failures are invisible — consequence: delete failures produce no feedback — smallest safe fix: await the futures inside try/catch and toast on error (same class as the delete-loop finding; fix at the handlers).
- [low] lib/features/settings/debug_log_screen.dart:32-42 — periodic 2-second timer calls `setState(() {})` unconditionally whenever `entryCount` changed, but `_lastEntryCount` is initialized to -1 and never reset on clear; after `clear()` the count drops and the timer keeps firing — consequence: minor: timer keeps screen alive and rebuilding even when idle; battery/CPU cost on a debug screen — smallest safe fix: cancel the timer when the screen is not visible or only run while mounted with a longer interval; (behavior is otherwise correct).
- [low] lib/features/settings/debug_log_screen.dart:126-129 — "Clear log" button awaits `_log.clear()` then `setState(() {})` without try/catch; if clear throws (e.g. file delete fails on some platforms) the exception escapes the onPressed handler — consequence: unhandled async error in release build — smallest safe fix: wrap in try/catch and toast on failure.
- [low] lib/features/settings/debug_log_screen.dart:67-97 — "Send to developer" inserts debug log content (which may include user prompts/media logged unredacted per service design) directly to Supabase `debug_reports` with no size cap or redaction — consequence: potentially large or sensitive log content uploaded without user confirmation beyond the tap — smallest safe fix: cap content length and confirm before upload (assumption: log content can contain arbitrary messages logged elsewhere).
- [info] lib/features/settings/debug_log_screen.dart:75-81 — verify the `debug_reports` insert is not committing user-identifying content (user_id, user_email, full log text) without tenant scoping; the code inserts `user_id`/`user_email` explicitly — consequence: if RLS is absent, any authenticated user could read other tenants' debug reports — smallest safe fix: verify Supabase RLS on `debug_reports` restricts rows to the inserting user (cannot confirm from inlined code).
- [info] lib/features/settings/ai_models_screen.dart:82 — `AnalyticsService.instance.logModelDownloaded(modelId: 'kokoro_onnx')` is fired after `tryLoadKokoro()` succeeds but before verifying the model actually initialized — consequence: analytics may report a successful download even if TTS init silently failed — smallest safe fix: log only after confirming load success (assumption: `tryLoadKokoro` returns a success indicator that is being ignored).
